# Remediation Tracker

Status values: `Not Started`, `In Progress`, `Blocked`, `Fixed`, `Verified`, `Won't Fix`.

Update this file when a future agent starts or finishes a slice. Keep evidence short and link the PR/commit when available.

| NOTIF-006 | P1 | Verified | Orders/Notifications | Verified direct refund/return endpoints and some post-sale state changes do not consistently enqueue customer-facing notification receipts, leaving buyers unaware of refund/return/payment-status progress. | Direct admin return/refund paths now emit explicit notification facts only after committed mutations: `processReturn()` returns previous/new status plus version for `order_returned`; `processRefund()` returns `order_refunded` for full refunds with `refund:${orderId}:${refundGroupId}:full`, `order_partially_refunded` for partial refunds with `refund:${orderId}:${refundGroupId}:partial`, and no fact for no-op repair results; API routes enqueue durable outbox rows without exposing internal notification facts. Balance-paid sub-slice adds explicit `payment_balance_paid` customer/admin event settings, customer email/SMS/WhatsApp templates, admin-push labeling, order-level dedupe `payment_balance_paid:{orderId}`, and confirmed Stripe/SSLCommerz/Polar `paymentType=balance` queue routing so remaining-payment receipts no longer collide with `order_created` dedupe. Customer timeline sub-slice centralizes account notification receipt projection, labels `SMS`/`WhatsApp` cleanly, and asserts that `payment_balance_paid`, `order_refunded`, and `order_partially_refunded` receipts become customer account timeline events. Polar external-refund sub-slice makes `processPolarWebhookRefund()` return a private full/partial refund notification fact only after local processing succeeds, uses stable `polar-refund:*` dedupe keys for idempotent full and cumulative partial webhooks, and has the payment queue consumer enqueue the fact through the durable outbox. Reconciler sub-slice makes `finalizeAcceptedRefundAttemptIds()` return ledger-derived full/partial refund notification facts and has scheduled maintenance record them through the same durable outbox after local reconciliation commits. Refund state-message sub-slice adds `refund_processing` and `refund_failed` customer/admin setting events, email/SMS/WhatsApp labels/templates, group-deduped reconciler facts for provider-pending and provider-rejected attempts, and keeps expired pre-provider `pending` attempts silent because no refund reached a gateway. Direct partial-failure sub-slice adds `PartialRefundProcessedError` so split direct refunds and auto-refunded returns carry committed finalizer facts, affected order IDs, optional `order_returned` status changes, and group-deduped remainder state facts to the API route; routes invalidate affected availability caches and enqueue those facts before returning the operator-facing failure. Stripe external-refund sub-slice keeps `charge.refunded` queue handling audit-only, marks the durable webhook row for manual reconciliation with charge/refund evidence, and has scheduled reconciliation import succeeded provider refunds into deterministic local refund rows before finalizing and emitting buyer notification facts. Admin delivery-state sub-slice exposes durable outbox rows plus channel delivery receipts on order detail, gates retry to failed/pending parent rows through the existing outbox enqueue helper, and binds retry to `orders.edit` while read-only history uses `orders.view`. Manual fulfillment sub-slice returns a private `statusChange` fact only when a final own-courier/manual shipment actually changes buyer-visible order status from `confirmed` to `shipped`; the API records that fact through the durable notification outbox and strips it from the admin response, while fulfillment-status-only edits do not reuse `order_completed`. Explicit terminal resend sub-slice adds `POST /api/v1/admin/orders/{id}/notifications/{outboxId}/resend`, gated by `orders.edit`, requiring a merchant/client `resendRequestId`, and creating a fresh `manual_resend:{sourceOutboxId}:{resendRequestId}` outbox parent only for `sent` notifications; retry remains limited to failed/pending/dead-lettered rows. | NOTIF-008 | P1 | Verified | Orders/Notifications | Live order `DW8W05` showed dummy/bad SMS.net.bd credentials causing hundreds of order-notification attempts, while old duplicate Queue retries bypassed the outbox backoff and surfaced noisy `delivery_receipt_busy` errors in admin order detail. | SMS provider results now classify merchant-actionable credential/config/account/recipient/balance failures as non-retryable skipped receipts, while transient HTTP/provider/network responses remain retryable. | PAY-008 | P1 | Verified | Payments/Webhooks | Cloudflare payment reliability research verified `webhook_events` can remain terminal `queued` after queue/DLQ/manual-reconciliation outcomes because payment queue messages did not carry webhook event IDs and consumers did not close the source event row. | Queue messages now carry `webhookEventId`, the consumer marks `processed`/`manual_reconciliation`/terminal `failed`, scheduled maintenance fails stale six-hour queued payment events through an indexed bounded sweep, the `payment-events-dlq` consumer archives compact D1 evidence without replaying payment side effects, and admin order payment history surfaces sanitized webhook issues. |
| PAY-009 | P1 | Verified | Payments/Refunds | Cloudflare payment reliability research verified ambiguous provider refund failures can be locally rolled back even if the provider accepted the refund, allowing duplicate refund attempts on retry. | Fail-closed slices now keep ambiguous online-provider failures pending, add D1 `refund_attempts` as the durable per-allocation operation ledger, block new refunds while active attempts exist, mark provider-successful/local-unreconciled attempts as `reconcile_required`, run a bounded scheduled reconciler that probes Stripe/SSLCommerz/Polar or locally finalizes accepted attempts before releasing claims, expose sanitized admin/customer recovery visibility, and add explicit admin manual refund-attempt recovery through `POST /api/v1/admin/orders/{id}/refund-attempts/{attemptId}/reconcile`. Manual recovery honors the same D1 claim lease as scheduled recovery, can force-check provider-visible active attempts before the next scheduled probe, refuses active leases, and does not prematurely fail fresh `pending` attempts before they are due. Admin payment detail now polls while active refund recovery exists, offers refund-capable operators a compact `Check now` action per active attempt, refreshes order/list/payment queries, and keeps failed attempts retryable through the normal `Issue Refund` path after the active lock clears. Verification: focused core/API/admin/RBAC tests, SDK regeneration (`272` OpenAPI paths), root `pnpm typecheck`, `pnpm lint`, `pnpm test` (`327` files / `2223` tests), `pnpm build`, `pnpm check:env`, `pnpm check:dist-secrets`, migration metadata guard, `git diff --check`, API deploy `0428a210-b70a-4265-8383-607fd0ba91cd`, admin deploy `fa6562c0-ec23-46cd-9d28-ce31f9740d1f`, live API health/readyz, live OpenAPI route smoke, and dashboard unauthenticated redirect smoke pass. |

Recovery lookup failures fail closed instead of risking duplicates. | PERF-012 | P1 | Verified | Checkout/Runtime Performance | Hot checkout/payment paths had avoidable serial work and stale client authority: order-session totals could drift from server totals before payment mode selection, cache prefix sweeps could hold payment processing, and checkout/payment paths lacked measured load proof. | First slice batches D1 reservation variant-state reads; `ARCH-004` then retired the legacy order-ingest consumer from Scalius API and removed that serial queue surface entirely. Checkout-latency slice makes `commitStorefrontOrderPayload()` D1-only, keeps order commit plus checkout-attempt finalization awaited, and moves `checkout_status:*` plus `order_receipt:*` KV recovery hints behind `waitUntil` after D1 authority is established; COD-only storefront success schedules abandoned-checkout cleanup with `Astro.locals.cfContext.waitUntil` when available. Latest self-heal/dedupe slice makes `/orders/status/:token` D1 fallbacks that prove committed/failed state repair `checkout_status:*` and completed `order_receipt:*` hints through the same guarded background writer, so transient KV misses do not cause repeated polling reads. It also short-circuits exact duplicate fresh `checkout_attempts` and `payment_session_attempts` processing rows from the first selected row instead of issuing a reclaim update and second state read. Target-gateway settings slice splits raw merchant allowlist/checkout-flow checks from provider readiness and fresh-reads only the selected Stripe/SSLCommerz/Polar credentials during payment-session creation, so unrelated gateways no longer add decrypt/cache/provider-read work or unrelated failure surface to a single buyer payment attempt. Customer account recovery slice makes order-detail `paymentRecovery` reuse the customer-owned order header instead of reloading the same order, keeps private recovery fields out of the public passthrough response, and routes `/customer-auth/orders/{id}/payment-session` through one request-local `createCustomerAccountPaymentSession()` dispatcher so order/gateway/policy/settings are resolved once before provider work. Receipt/public-order boundary slice makes receipt pages pass their already loaded order state into support-action resolution, filters deleted orders in the receipt order read, serializes receipt DTOs explicitly so internal `customerId`/`fulfillmentStatus` fields do not leak, repairs `order_receipt:*` KV only for committed checkout-attempt fallbacks, and removes the app-level bearer middleware from public `/orders/*` proof/origin-guarded routes after live smoke proved valid receipt links were incorrectly returning `401`. Final side-effect/logging slice makes Stripe, SSLCommerz, and Polar session creation prove selected gateway readiness before inserting a deposit payment plan, so dummy/bad credentials fail closed without payment-plan or payment-session-attempt writes; expected typed readiness failures now log as compact one-line `[api-error]` warnings instead of stack traces while unknown crashes still keep full error objects. Verification: parallel PERF-012 subagent audits, focused core/API/admin/storefront tests from prior slices, affected core/API/admin typecheck+lint, root gates from prior slices, local COD load proof through `scripts/dev-postsale.mjs` (`50/50` `201`, concurrency `5`, p95 `181.5ms`, zero failures), focused payment-session/api-response test suite (`72` files / `608` tests), API typecheck/lint, script syntax/focused tests (`22` tests), root typecheck/lint/test/build (`316` files / `2133` tests), env/dist-secret/migration/diff guards, local `payment-readiness` smoke proving unconfigured Stripe/SSLCommerz/Polar return `503` with `0` `payment_plans` and `0` `payment_session_attempts` plus one-line warnings, API deploy `da0da14c-9c9e-4f00-9595-1f1a26fa07b6`, and live read-only API health/readyz/checkout-config/OpenAPI smokes (`269` paths). Prior deploys: API `4cddbd1f-24af-4376-a580-a970edf2deca`, target-gateway API `a0dcce84-09ba-4faa-bc6f-07d7936c341b`, customer-account recovery API `f77eec45-ac5d-40d7-9d44-d049c73e7bfd`, receipt/public-order boundary API `ad652836-3aea-420b-940d-77db538f71c0`. |

## Remediation Notes

2026-07-05:

- `PRIV-003` is verified and deployed. Guest receipt proof now stays out of browser-visible URLs: storefront checkout/COD/gateway flows set a per-order host-only `HttpOnly` receipt cookie, `/order-success` ignores and strips legacy `token`/`receipt_token`/`receiptToken` query params, receipt API reads require `X-Receipt-Token`, same-origin payment/support proxies forward proof from the cookie, SSLCommerz/Polar callback redirects and provider URLs are proof-free, and admin payment-recovery copy links return clean `existing_browser_receipt` URLs without `receiptToken`/`tokenHash`. Receipt KV hints now use `order_receipt:{sha256(token)}` instead of raw `chk_` keys. Verification: parallel API/storefront workers plus integration review, SDK regeneration, focused API/admin/storefront/core tests, root `pnpm typecheck`, `pnpm lint`, `pnpm test` (`354` files / `2398` tests), `pnpm build`, `pnpm check:env`, `pnpm check:dist-secrets`, `git diff --check`, local dev API/storefront receipt smokes, API deploy `72f559fd-dbf9-40ac-9e29-eea0a9a50ce6`, storefront deploy `7f448241-8ef3-46aa-abe3-dd7277568cb9`, admin deploy `3cc22699-379c-452c-8d77-d2147bb80826`, live API `/health`/`readyz`, live receipt query proof `404`, live tokenized `/order-success` redirecting to clean `/order-success?orderId=...`, live rendered recovery page with zero proof markers, live OpenAPI receipt header proof, and live admin recovery schema with `existing_browser_receipt` and no token fields. Remaining post-sale product scope: `POSTSALE-021` buyer-verified cross-browser recovery, because merchant-sendable recovery must not reintroduce a renamed bearer URL.
- `POSTSALE-021` is verified and deployed. Buyer cross-browser hosted-payment recovery now uses a proofless `/payment-recovery?orderId=...` URL plus an order-owned D1 `order_payment_recovery_challenges` table (`0078`) with hashed identifiers/codes, cooldowns, attempts, expiry, queue handoff cleanup, and bounded scheduled cleanup. Admin recovery copy now returns a buyer-verification link with `accessMode: "buyer_verified_receipt"` and no raw receipt token/hash. Public `POST /orders/payment-recovery/send-otp` returns generic no-store success and never exposes contact/proof; protected `POST /orders/payment-recovery/verify-otp` is service-JWT-only for the storefront server handoff. Storefront `/payment-recovery` is POST-only, verifies buyer contact by SMS/WhatsApp/email, then the same-origin proxy sets the existing per-order HttpOnly receipt cookie and returns a clean `/order-success?...` redirect without putting proof in browser JSON or URLs. Order-success missing-cookie hosted-payment fallback now links to recovery and still renders no receipt PII. Purpose-specific OTP delivery copy uses `auth.send_otp` with `purpose: "order_payment_recovery"`. Verification: three parallel audit reports in `audit/parallel/POSTSALE-021-*.md`, focused core/API/admin/storefront tests, `pnpm --filter @scalius/api typecheck`, `pnpm --filter @scalius/storefront typecheck`, `pnpm --filter @scalius/admin-v2 typecheck`, `pnpm generate:sdk`, `pnpm --filter @scalius/database check:migrations`, root `pnpm typecheck`, `pnpm lint`, `pnpm test` (`358` files / `2412` tests), `pnpm build`, `pnpm check:env`, `pnpm check:dist-secrets`, `git diff --check`, local D1 migration, local browser screenshot `/tmp/scalius-payment-recovery-local.png`, local recovery/missing-cookie HTTP smokes, remote D1 migration `0078`, full deploy API `af710575-999a-456c-ac1f-2bcf1925a9ae`, admin `08a844fc-39fb-4b10-9058-4f4111458ca9`, storefront `a0577540-d33b-4954-93af-614a060ed55e`, ops-monitor `5113b0ba-feef-45ee-8c99-95aed1cd82c6`, deploy-script `/health` and four `/readyz` samples, live `/readyz` `200`, live recovery page `200`, live missing-cookie order-success recovery state `400`, live API send-OTP generic `200`, live storefront proxy send-code same-origin `200`, direct verify `401`, and live proof-marker scans across recovery HTML, order-success HTML, and JSON responses showing no `chk_`, `receiptToken`, or `tokenHash`. Tooling note: `pnpm db:generate` was attempted before the manual migration but Drizzle reported the existing `0045`/`0047` snapshot parent collision and emitted no migration; manual migration metadata is allowlisted and `check:migrations` passes.
- `PERF-003` discount type selector slice removes the decorative `motion/react` dependency from `/admin/discounts/new` type selection. `DiscountTypeSelector` now uses Tailwind hover/active/focus-visible transitions while keeping button semantics and route-owned lazy form loading. Live smoke: `/api/scanner-token` no-cookie `401`, invalid token `401`, no-cookie POST `401`, unauthenticated `/admin` `307`, demo sign-in `200`, and authenticated `/admin` `200`. This is not full auth-route isolation: `/api/auth/*` and scanner-token session lookup still dynamically import `auth.server`, which should remain a separate auth architecture slice if pursued.

- `CHECKOUT-011` fresh parallel audit found a P1 duplicate-order risk: hosted SSLCommerz/Polar flows could commit an online order, clear checkout session state on redirect/recovery, and leave the local cart able to generate a fresh `checkoutId`, bypassing backend same-key replay and creating a second incomplete order for the same cart.
- `CHECKOUT-011` hosted cart-consumption slice is verified and deployed. SSLCommerz and Polar now set `clearCartOnRedirect` after a durable order commit for both external gateway redirects and receipt recovery redirects, so stale local cart data cannot create a second incomplete order with a fresh checkout id after the first order exists. - `PERF-014` is verified. Cart runtime initialization is now idempotent across repeated `initCartFunctionality()` and Astro page transitions: the previous store subscription is unsubscribed, DOM listeners are owned by an `AbortController`, abandoned-checkout timers are cleared before rewire, duplicate discount/checkout listeners are prevented, and `document`-scoped `cart-updated` events refresh checkout state correctly. Cart page-owned listeners are also abortable, stale cart repair content renders before checkout actions on mobile, and leaving the cart clears only checkout transfer/session payloads while preserving the active abandoned-checkout id used for recovery. Verification: focused cart/session tests, root gates, local Astro dev desktop/mobile cart browser smokes, and generated Worker cart smoke pass.

Credentialed API CORS no longer reads merchant/admin CSP domains or CDN domains from KV/runtime settings; `getCorsOriginContext()` now allows local dev, first-party runtime origins, and explicit exact URL origins from credentialed-CORS env vars only, while filtering wildcard env entries. Unsafe browser requests that carry cookies are now rejected when `Origin` is not first-party/exact-allowed: the API guard covers customer-auth, cache, orders, and admin routes; admin same-origin file-route proxies guard admin API, auth, and scanner-token writes; storefront customer-auth/logout/checkout/payment-session proxies guard buyer writes. The admin Security UI now labels these domains as CSP-only so merchants do not think they are configuring credentialed API access. - `SEC-007` is verified and deployed. Admin `GET /api/v1/admin/abandoned-checkouts` no longer deletes old abandoned-checkout rows, archives stale hosted-payment orders, or prunes empty rows while serving a list read. This removes the hidden mutation from an `ORDERS_VIEW` route and avoids the previous GET path's missing cache-invalidation side effect. - `AUTH-025` is verified and deployed. Customer OTP verification authority moved from read-modify-write KV JSON to D1 `customer_auth_otp_challenges`: `sendOtp()` now stores only an HMAC-SHA256 code hash plus channel/intent/pinned-contact metadata, `/send-otp` deletes the exact challenge on queue handoff failure, `verifyOtp()` uses guarded D1 updates to atomically consume correct codes or increment/lock wrong-code attempts, and scheduled maintenance deletes expired/stale challenge rows in bounded batches. We intentionally do not restore a used OTP after account-policy errors, because restoring correct-code state was one of the race paths. - Admin Checkout Flow/Auth readiness red-state check: production checkout delivery readiness is healthy by read-only remote D1 and public config evidence (`3` active shipping methods, `1258` active city-zone pairs, `/api/v1/checkout/config` has `checkoutReadiness.ready=true`). The confusing delivery rows were first made amber instead of hard-red when the admin readiness check was unavailable; the exact follow-up root cause was `/api/v1/admin/settings/checkout-readiness` missing from the central RBAC route map, so authenticated settings viewers received fail-closed `403` even though checkout itself was ready. `RBAC-003` maps the endpoint to `settings.general.view` and is deployed in API version `cfd3f7db-c8a8-47dc-9e0d-b461b60ff22d`. A 2026-06-20 recheck after the screenshot reconfirmed the public checkout side is ready, and the admin UI now refetches checkout readiness on mount/focus, shows a loading state while retrying, and surfaces the actual API error if the admin-side read still fails. A later 2026-06-20 screenshot recheck found the live authenticated admin proxy and public checkout config both returning `checkoutReadiness.ready=true`; the remaining weakness was the Checkout Flow client using TanStack server-function transport for a tiny browser-only status read, where non-browser-like/referrer-stripped requests could return `403` and leave the panel in the amber fallback. The Checkout Flow readiness query now uses `/api/v1/admin/settings/checkout-readiness` through the existing dashboard admin proxy in the browser, keeps the server-function path only for server-side execution, and changes the amber copy to "Checkout readiness status could not be refreshed" so merchants do not mistake a status-check failure for missing shipping setup. Admin deploy `8e0ef4c2-2370-4b88-8e6c-a9a9a95c767b`; live smokes verified dashboard proxy readiness `200`, public checkout config readiness `true`, and deployed Checkout Flow copy/asset markers. Auth & Access SMS red state is a real fail-closed provider readiness issue when SMS is selected: stored SMS.net.bd credentials exist but cannot be decrypted by the current credential key, while the live customer auth policy remains email-only. The UI now explains that merchants must re-enter and save the provider API key or restore the original `CREDENTIAL_ENCRYPTION_KEY`. Verified conclusions opened `ARCH-001`, `PAY-008`, and `PAY-009`; `CACHE-016` remains the tracked cache durability gap. Near-term direction is D1 as the authoritative ledger for checkout receipts, sessions, webhook/refund/payment attempts, Queues only after durable claims, KV as cache/status hints, Service Bindings for internal Worker control paths, and narrow Durable Objects only for per-checkout/per-entity coordination where measured contention or idempotency requires strict serialization.

- `ORDER-017` is verified and deployed. Order status inventory transitions now write deterministic, movement-generation-based `transition:*` movement claims and variant counter CAS updates in one D1 `safeBatch()` for deduct/release/restore, adopting exact duplicate claims as idempotent retry success and rejecting mismatched duplicate claims for manual reconciliation. The claim generation is derived from prior movement history instead of `orders.version`, so unrelated order edits cannot make a crash retry double-apply stock. - `ORDER-015` is verified and deployed. `releaseExpiredReservations()` now sweeps a bounded orphan-reservation batch (`50` default, max `200`) and returns `hasMore` for backlog visibility. Each expired release uses deterministic movement id `expiry_release:{orderId}:{variantId}` and applies the movement claim plus reserved-stock decrement through D1 `safeBatch()`, so overlapping cron runs cannot double-release the same orphan group; stale active orders still remain outside the sweeper and must use explicit order transition logic. The API scheduled handler logs `limit` and `hasMore`. Verification: `pnpm --filter @scalius/core test -- src/modules/inventory/expiry.test.ts` and `pnpm --filter @scalius/core typecheck`.
- `ORDER-002` fixed by moving provider shipment creation behind an order-version claim, removing the direct admin shipment route's low-level `createShipment()` bypass, moving COD collection/return writes after successful order CAS, and delaying manual-fulfillment inventory transitions until after the fulfillment claim and shipment/item writes. Verification: `pnpm --filter @scalius/core test -- src/modules/orders/orders.fulfillment.test.ts`, `pnpm --filter @scalius/core typecheck`, `pnpm --filter @scalius/api typecheck`, and `git diff --check`.
- `ORDER-003` fixed by reserving stock per order inside `handleOrderIngestBatch()`, removing reservation-failed order writes before the DB batch, retrying only the message whose reservation failed, and falling back to isolated per-order reserve/write/ack handling after a shared DB batch failure. Verification: `pnpm --filter @scalius/core test -- src/modules/orders/orders.queue.test.ts`, `pnpm --filter @scalius/core typecheck`, and `git diff --check`.
- `ORDER-004` fixed by writing checkout polling KV and receipt-token KV before `ORDER_INGEST_QUEUE.send()`, rewriting checkout status to `failed` if enqueue fails after KV creation, and routing order-ingest queue batches by the actual queue name (`order-ingest`, plus legacy alias) instead of broad mixed-batch body detection. Verification: `pnpm --filter @scalius/api test -- src/routes/orders-create.test.ts src/queue-consumer.test.ts`, `pnpm --filter @scalius/api typecheck`, and `git diff --check`.
- `ORDER-005` fixed by replacing direct hard deletion of stale incomplete orders in admin abandoned-checkout cleanup with `archiveStaleIncompleteOrders()`: reserved/deducted orders first release inventory via `applyInventoryForStatusChange(db, orderId, OrderStatus.CANCELLED)`, then archive an abandoned-checkout snapshot, then mark the order cancelled/restored and soft-deleted. If inventory release fails, the order/items remain present for retry and movement links are not nulled by a hard delete. Current verification entry points are `pnpm --filter @scalius/api test -- src/routes/admin/system-utils-safe-methods.test.ts src/scheduled-maintenance.test.ts`, `pnpm --filter @scalius/core test -- src/modules/orders/stale-incomplete-orders.test.ts src/modules/abandoned-checkout/abandoned-checkout-cleanup.test.ts`, `pnpm --filter @scalius/api typecheck`, and `pnpm --filter @scalius/api lint`.
- `ORDER-012` fixed by making abandoned-checkout cleanup win a version/status/payment-state/stale-cutoff claim before releasing inventory, skip active shipment claims and pending/succeeded payment records, roll the cleanup claim back when inventory release fails, finalize soft-delete with a second guard, and write the archive row only after finalization. Also fixed the live admin SSR failure by skipping server-side prefetch on the abandoned-checkouts route's browser-proxy-only relative fetch. Verification: focused API abandoned-checkout tests, API/admin typechecks and lints, API/admin builds, root `pnpm test`, env/dist-secret checks, API deploy version `1ab100f5-5e2b-4d8a-9fb3-c7ad0a4a3a07`, admin deploy version `af228559-8956-434c-bda7-88ae511cf4c9`, live API/auth/session/admin route/proxy/storefront HTTP smoke, dashboard browser smoke, and storefront browser smoke.
- `ORDER-013` fixed by changing manual fulfillment to acquire a private shipment claim before visible status changes, validate non-empty unique same-order item IDs, scope item updates by `orderId`, and batch the manual shipment insert, item updates, and final order status/fulfillment/claim-clear update together. Batch failure before shipment insert clears the private claim and leaves inventory untouched. Verification: focused core fulfillment tests, core/API typechecks, core lint, API dry-run build, root `pnpm test`, env/dist-secret checks, API deploy version `37698b5e-89fe-4978-b8df-2e94145021c3`, live API/OpenAPI/auth/admin/storefront HTTP smoke, dashboard orders browser smoke, and storefront browser smoke.
- `ORDER-014` fixed by removing COD auto-pay from generic order status updates. Generic COD `delivered`/`completed` transitions now require successful COD payment evidence, collected COD tracking, `paymentStatus = paid`, positive `paidAmount`, and zero balance; otherwise callers must use the COD collection action that writes `cod_tracking`, `order_payments`, and order payment totals together. Verification: focused core fulfillment/COD status tests, core/API typechecks, core lint, API dry-run build, root `pnpm test`, env/dist-secret checks, API deploy version `37698b5e-89fe-4978-b8df-2e94145021c3`, live API/OpenAPI/auth/admin/storefront HTTP smoke, dashboard orders browser smoke, and storefront browser smoke.
- `STORE-005` fixed by routing checkout config inline-script serialization through `serializeJsonForInlineScript()`, escaping `<`, `>`, `&`, U+2028, and U+2029 while preserving parseable JSON. Verification: focused safe-json DOM-parser breakout test, storefront typecheck/build, storefront lint, root `pnpm test`, `pnpm check:dist-secrets`, `git diff --check`, storefront deploy version `3215e5be-1237-47bd-a2b1-ac92c3805a58`, live `/checkout` HTML check for `window.__CHECKOUT_CONFIG__`, and browser smoke with no console or Worker-tail errors.
- `STORE-006` fixed by moving the empty-cart state renderer into `apps/storefront/src/lib/cart/empty-state.ts` and assigning active checkout-language strings through `textContent`/text nodes instead of `innerHTML`. Verification: happy-dom malicious language-string test, focused checkout XSS test set, storefront typecheck/build, storefront lint, root `pnpm test`, `pnpm check:dist-secrets`, `git diff --check`, storefront deploy version `3215e5be-1237-47bd-a2b1-ac92c3805a58`, and live browser inspection of `#cartItems` showing the empty-cart renderer with no injected `img`/`script` nodes.
- `PRIV-004` fixed by removing cart-side standalone `scalius_user_*` analytics PII capture, limiting Meta CAPI default browser user data to `_fbp`, `_fbc`, and user agent, requiring event-specific PII to be explicitly supplied, extending checkout cleanup to remove legacy PII keys, and clearing raw checkout transfer state before SSLCommerz/Polar external redirects while leaving cart intact for cancel/failure recovery. Verification: focused Meta CAPI/session/checkout redirect tests, storefront typecheck/build/lint, all-workspace typecheck during deploy, root `pnpm test` (105 files, 703 tests), `pnpm check:dist-secrets`, `git diff --check`, storefront deploy version `4391b4cd-7a08-438c-be2e-193d8df7a79e`, live HTTP checks for `/`, `/cart`, and `/checkout`, live browser smoke with no console errors, and deployed asset scans confirming the analytics bundle has no legacy session-PII enrichment references.
- `CACHE-001` fixed by making payment-method, Stripe, SSLCommerz, and Polar settings saves invalidate API checkout KV prefixes and purge storefront checkout prefixes, adding `/polar` to checkout cache group routing, aligning the dormant core cache-invalidation copy, and making public checkout config honor `payment_methods.enabled_methods` as the outer gateway allowlist while still requiring each gateway's own settings to be enabled/configured. Verification: focused API payment-settings/cache-invalidation tests, focused core checkout-config tests, API/core typechecks and lints, all-workspace typecheck plus API dry-run build during deploy, root `pnpm test` (107 files, 710 tests), `pnpm check:env`, `pnpm check:dist-secrets`, `git diff --check`, API deploy version `22831cca-fe5d-4df0-a6d7-96bf3237a0ab`, live API health and `/api/v1/checkout/config` smoke, and storefront browser smoke with no console errors.
- `BUILD-005` fixed by adding app-local `.dev.vars`/`.env*` files to Turbo `globalDependencies`, adding build-time env names to `globalEnv`, and adding a regression test for those cache inputs. Verification: `scripts/turbo-config.test.mjs`, Turbo build dry-run JSON showing env files and configured env values in `globalCacheInputs`, root `pnpm test` (115 files, 748 tests), root `pnpm typecheck`, and `git diff --check`.
- `SDK-001` fixed by removing the deprecated `@hey-api/client-fetch` runtime dependency, updating `@hey-api/openapi-ts` to 0.98.2, deleting the post-generation `client-core` shim, importing the generated bundled client directly, and regenerating the SDK from a live local API worker. The regenerated OpenAPI artifact also catches up to the secured 2FA `complete-verification` contract. Verification: npm version/deprecation checks, `pnpm generate:sdk`, API-client/admin/storefront focused typechecks, root `pnpm typecheck`, root `pnpm test` (115 files, 748 tests), root `pnpm lint`, `pnpm audit --audit-level moderate`, and `git diff --check`.
- `PAY-001` fixed by moving Stripe and SSLCommerz webhook ingress to the existing durable `webhook_events` claim-before-enqueue helper, marking queue failures as retryable failed claims, and removing KV-only post-send dedupe. `processPaymentFailed()` now records/claims failed attempts before order updates, duplicate failed attempts no-op, failed attempts can be promoted by later successful gateway events, and payment cancellation releases inventory through the centralized idempotent inventory transition. Verification: `pnpm --filter @scalius/api test -- src/routes/webhooks/stripe.test.ts src/routes/webhooks/sslcommerz.test.ts src/routes/webhooks/polar.test.ts`, `pnpm --filter @scalius/core test -- src/modules/payments/process-payment.test.ts`, `pnpm --filter @scalius/api typecheck`, and `pnpm --filter @scalius/core typecheck`.
- `PAY-002` fixed by making Polar webhook refunds CAS-update `orders.paymentStatus`, `orders.paidAmount`, and allowed `orders.status` transitions before inventory release. Pre-fulfillment full refunds now transition to `cancelled` and release reservations, fulfilled full refunds can transition to `refunded` without auto-restocking, and old rows with `paymentStatus=refunded` but stale order status are repaired on redelivery. Verification: `pnpm --filter @scalius/core test -- src/modules/payments/polar.test.ts src/modules/payments/process-payment.test.ts` and `pnpm --filter @scalius/core typecheck`.
- `PAY-003` fixed by extracting shared receipt-token validation, requiring payment-session `receiptToken` for Stripe/SSLCommerz/Polar, rejecting missing or foreign tokens before gateway settings/provider calls, matching the requested gateway to the order payment method, rejecting invalid Polar paid/cancelled/returned/deposit/balance sessions, deriving SSLCommerz/Polar gateway callback URLs from trusted `PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL`, and regenerating the API client so caller URL fields are no longer advertised. Verification: `pnpm --filter @scalius/api test -- src/routes/payment/payment-session.test.ts src/routes/orders-receipt.test.ts`, `pnpm generate:sdk` against a live local API worker, focused API/storefront/api-client typechecks, and focused API/storefront/api-client lint.
- `PAY-004` fixed by routing Stripe, SSLCommerz, and Polar session creation through a shared server-side payment-session policy. Deposit payments now require enabled partial payment settings and must match the configured partial amount, balance payments require server-side balance state, caller currency is ignored in favor of configured currency, and Stripe manual capture is forced off for public sessions. Verification: `pnpm --filter @scalius/api exec vitest run src/routes/payment/payment-session.test.ts src/routes/webhooks/sslcommerz.test.ts` and `pnpm --filter @scalius/api typecheck`.
- `PAY-005` fixed by making SSLCommerz IPN validate `val_id` first, derive canonical `tran_id`/`val_id`/payment fields from the validation response, claim durable webhook events with canonical identifiers, and resolve payment type against server-side order/payment-plan state instead of trusting form metadata. Verification: `pnpm --filter @scalius/api exec vitest run src/routes/payment/payment-session.test.ts src/routes/webhooks/sslcommerz.test.ts` and `pnpm --filter @scalius/api typecheck`.
- `PAY-007` fixed by using unique scoped SSLCommerz merchant `tran_id` values per full/deposit/balance attempt, carrying canonical order ids through trusted callback `order_id` and validated `value_b`, queueing IPNs with canonical order ids plus full attempt ids, moving confirmed SSLCommerz idempotency to `sslcommerz_val_id`, and normalizing completed payment plans to `completed`. Verification: focused API payment-session/webhook tests, focused core payment processor tests, API/core/database typechecks and lints, database migration metadata check, root `pnpm test`, `pnpm check:env`, `pnpm check:dist-secrets`, API deploy version `9a25a484-b501-4f84-88f5-f3bf4a0b0eba`, live API/OpenAPI/auth HTTP checks, authenticated dashboard `/admin` + `/admin/orders` browser smoke, and storefront browser smoke.
- `ORDER-006` fixed by requiring an active, non-deleted shipping method whenever shipping applies and deriving shipping charge from the selected method fee. Free-delivery product behavior still explicitly waives shipping method and charge. Verification: `pnpm --filter @scalius/core test -- src/modules/orders/orders.storefront.test.ts` and `pnpm --filter @scalius/core typecheck`.
- `ORDER-007` fixed by making status/fulfillment/COD/delivery/refund/return paths reconcile inventory on same-status retries instead of returning before `applyInventoryForStatusChange()`. Delivery webhooks and shipment refreshes now invoke `updateOrderStatusFromShipment()` even when the shipment row already has the provider status, while notifications remain gated on actual order status changes. Already-cancelled full-refund retries release only pre-fulfillment/non-deducted inventory and do not auto-restore fulfilled refunds. Verification: `pnpm --filter @scalius/core exec vitest run src/modules/orders/orders.fulfillment.test.ts src/modules/delivery/tracking.test.ts src/modules/payments/polar.test.ts src/modules/payments/process-payment.test.ts`, `pnpm exec vitest run tests/unit/core/orders/update-order-atomicity.test.ts tests/unit/core/payments/refund-validation.test.ts`, `pnpm --filter @scalius/api exec vitest run src/routes/webhooks/steadfast.test.ts src/routes/admin/system-utils-safe-methods.test.ts src/scheduled-maintenance.test.ts`, `pnpm --filter @scalius/core typecheck`, `pnpm --filter @scalius/api typecheck`, `pnpm --filter @scalius/core lint`, `pnpm --filter @scalius/api lint`, and `git diff --check`.
- `WEBHOOK-001` fixed by adding a five-minute processing lease to `apps/api/src/utils/webhook-idempotency.ts`. Fresh `processing` claims still dedupe, stale `processing` claims are atomically reclaimable by one retry, `failed` claims remain atomically reclaimable, `queued`/`processed` remain terminal for claim purposes, and insert failures with no existing row now throw so providers receive retryable failures instead of false duplicates. Verification: `pnpm --filter @scalius/api exec vitest run src/utils/webhook-idempotency.test.ts src/routes/webhooks/stripe.test.ts src/routes/webhooks/sslcommerz.test.ts src/routes/webhooks/polar.test.ts src/routes/webhooks/steadfast.test.ts`, `pnpm --filter @scalius/api typecheck`, `pnpm --filter @scalius/api lint`, and `git diff --check`.
- `DEL-001` fixed by moving Pathao and Steadfast delivery webhooks to durable `webhook_events` claims before shipment/order side effects, making Steadfast event identity include delivery status or tracking update identity, and expanding `updateOrderStatusFromShipment()` to handle emitted canonical statuses (`pickup_assigned`, `out_for_delivery`, `partial_delivered`, `pickup_failed`, `delivery_failed`, `on_hold`, `unknown`). Failed carrier delivery retry semantics now explicitly allow `shipped -> confirmed`, and direct `confirmed -> delivered` webhooks deduct reserved inventory by treating `delivered` as a stock-deducting transition. Verification: `pnpm --filter @scalius/api test -- src/routes/webhooks/steadfast.test.ts`, `pnpm --filter @scalius/core test -- src/modules/delivery/tracking.test.ts`, `pnpm --filter @scalius/api typecheck`, and `pnpm --filter @scalius/core typecheck`.
- `STORE-001` fixed by making selective storefront prefix purges bump the KV cache version used by L2 Cache API keys. Checkout invalidation can keep `bumpVersion: false` from the API side, but the storefront policy still bumps the global version and warms critical pages when prefixes are present. Verification: `pnpm --filter @scalius/storefront exec vitest run src/lib/cache-purge-policy.test.ts --passWithNoTests`, `pnpm --filter @scalius/api test -- src/utils/cache-invalidation.test.ts`, `pnpm --filter @scalius/storefront typecheck`, and `pnpm --filter @scalius/api typecheck`.
- `OPS-001` fixed by making storefront `typecheck` generate `src/config/build-id.ts` before `astro check` and changing `scripts/generate-build-id.js` from timestamp-only output to deterministic commit/source-hash output. Clean deploy is covered because root deploy runs `pnpm typecheck` before build. Verification: ran the generator twice and confirmed the same `BUILD_ID`, then ran `pnpm --filter @scalius/storefront typecheck`.
- `OPS-002` fixed by routing root `deploy:api`, `deploy:admin`, and `deploy:storefront` through `scripts/deploy.mjs --only ...`; targeted deploys typecheck before building/deploying, and the API target applies remote D1 migrations first. Verification: `node --check scripts/deploy.mjs`, invalid target guard, and script inspection.
- `OPS-003` fixed by making `scripts/deploy.mjs --dry-run` a real non-mutating mode. Dry runs still typecheck, build, and run dist-secret checks, but they skip D1 migrations and Worker deploys for targeted and full deploys; migration-only dry runs print the intended target without applying migrations. Verification: `node --check scripts/deploy.mjs` and `pnpm run deploy:api -- --dry-run`.
- `TEST-001` fixed by making `parseGeneratedWidgetContent()` return normalized widget parts so local-safe HTML-owned script blocks move into widget JS before preview. Queue retry metadata now writes generic buyer-facing retry reasons, and the checkout DOM test runs under `happy-dom`. Verification: focused widget/queue/checkout tests plus root `pnpm test`; current root baseline is recorded above.
- `DEPLOY-001` fixed by detecting recoverable admin route-load failures such as stale dynamic-import/chunk errors, auto-reloading once per route/error signature, and falling back to an explicit reload action instead of leaving users on the generic 500 screen. Dashboard analytics date filters now compare Unix timestamps against Unix timestamp columns. Verification: focused admin route-error and scanner-token tests, admin/core typechecks, admin build, root `pnpm test`, root `pnpm lint`, full `pnpm run deploy`, live API/admin/storefront HTTP checks, browser checks for `https://dashboard.scalius.com/admin` and `https://storefront.scalius.com/`, and `wrangler tail` confirmation that the redeployed admin Worker served `/admin`, `_serverFn`, and `/api/auth/get-session` without exceptions.
- `PERF-003` first slice split the admin dashboard into `/api/v1/admin/dashboard/summary` and `/api/v1/admin/dashboard/activity`, kept the legacy combined endpoint, changed the admin home loader to await only summary data and prefetch activity in the background, added `customers_dashboard_activity_idx` in migration `0041_fixed_whistler.sql`, and regenerated the production-aligned SDK to 254 paths / 352 operations. Verification: `pnpm exec vitest run apps/api/src/routes/admin/dashboard.test.ts`, `pnpm --filter @scalius/database check:migrations`, affected workspace typechecks/lints, root `pnpm test`, root `pnpm typecheck`, root `pnpm build`, local API/admin/storefront browser smoke, `pnpm dev:doctor:all`, full deploy to API `d25ebe15-971e-409e-bcfd-b5d15c6702e6`, admin `3af1362d-cfe5-4722-94f6-0a3c44a5f6ae`, storefront `8976c406-b367-40d8-a39c-a795bd5d6862`, remote D1 index check, live OpenAPI count check, live authenticated split endpoint checks, and live browser `/admin`, `/admin/orders`, and storefront smoke.
- `PERF-003` second slice moved admin order-detail payment history, currency, and COD tracking warming into `apps/admin-v2/src/lib/order-detail-prefetch.ts`. The route now warms order, shipments, delivery providers, payment history, currency settings, and COD tracking for COD orders before render, while optional payment/COD/currency prefetch failures log and continue instead of redirecting away from an otherwise valid order. Component-level order invalidation now uses centralized `queryKeys`, and mounted order/payment/COD observers keep warmed data fresh for the first mount window. - `PERF-003` third slice trimmed checkout settings route work and started the query-options barrel split for hot shell paths. `/admin/settings/checkout` now preloads only auth settings, `CheckoutFlowSettings` consumes the shared TanStack Query auth-settings cache instead of a duplicate direct fetch, and payment/shipping tab data lazy-loads when those tabs are opened. Always-mounted sidebar, currency, storefront URL, and Firebase-init hooks now import narrow modules under `apps/admin-v2/src/lib/api-query-options/` instead of the broad `api.queries.ts` barrel. Verification: `pnpm exec vitest run apps/admin-v2/src/routes/admin/settings/-checkout-loader.test.ts`, broad-barrel import scan over the checkout/shell hot files, `pnpm --filter @scalius/admin-v2 typecheck`, `pnpm --filter @scalius/admin-v2 lint`, root `pnpm test` (121 files, 771 tests), root `pnpm typecheck`, root `pnpm lint`, root `pnpm build`, `pnpm check:env`, `pnpm check:dist-secrets`, `pnpm audit --audit-level moderate`, `git diff --check`, `pnpm outdated -r` (only storefront `vite` 7.3.5 -> 8.0.16, held because Astro 6.4.6 / `@astrojs/cloudflare` 13.7.0 depend on Vite `^7.3.2`), local `pnpm dev:doctor --require-running`, local browser checkout lazy-load/dashboard/storefront smoke, full deploy to API `d643773b-9e7b-4a9a-b0a7-7725245bd56f`, admin `f31f89ed-909f-4da3-8bba-084a0ea88d12`, storefront `400924eb-01a3-47b5-bb79-8cd5a172dd6e`, live API/storefront/dashboard HTTP smoke, live demo sign-in API check, and live browser `/admin`, `/admin/orders`, `/admin/settings/checkout`, storefront home/cart/checkout checks with no captured console errors.
- `PERF-003` fourth slice reduced admin list-navigation blocking without trading away freshness. The `/admin` route now uses a 15-second client-side `adminRouteGuard()` context cache that is primed from SSR/hydration and cleared on sign-out. Common list loaders use `warmRouteQuery()` so server rendering still awaits data, while cold client navigations prefetch in the background instead of blocking the route transition. Data tables keep previous rows visible and refetch on mount, and `api.queries.ts` lazy-loads domain server-function modules instead of importing every domain at module load. Verification: `pnpm --filter @scalius/admin-v2 typecheck`, `pnpm --filter @scalius/admin-v2 lint`, `pnpm exec vitest run apps/admin-v2/src/routes/admin/settings/-checkout-loader.test.ts apps/admin-v2/src/lib/admin-access.test.ts apps/admin-v2/src/lib/api.server.test.ts apps/api/src/routes/admin/auth-management.test.ts`, root `pnpm typecheck`, root `pnpm build`, root `pnpm test` (121 files, 773 tests), `pnpm check:env`, `pnpm check:dist-secrets`, `git diff --check`, full deploy to API `f8513a65-a8e8-463f-98aa-831ca5e3c5a8`, admin `550835d7-3075-486f-88b8-2c07960494be`, storefront `3a8a0294-239d-41c6-9aad-b7c8dd457601`, live API health/storefront home/dashboard sign-in checks, and live browser `/admin`, `/admin/products`, `/admin/orders`, `/admin/customers` smoke.
- `PERF-003` fifth slice fixed freshness gaps introduced or exposed by faster navigation. Profile and 2FA account settings now clear the client admin route-context cache before invalidating the route, so shell/header state refreshes immediately after current-user changes. Product/customer/order mutation hooks now invalidate `queryKeys.dashboard.all`, category mutations invalidate `queryKeys.products.stats()`, direct category creation paths match that policy, and bulk shipment from the orders list invalidates order list/detail/shipment/dashboard keys after successful shipment creation. Verification: `pnpm --filter @scalius/admin-v2 typecheck`, `pnpm --filter @scalius/admin-v2 lint`, `pnpm typecheck`, `pnpm lint`, `pnpm test` (121 files, 773 tests), `pnpm build`, `pnpm check:env`, `pnpm check:dist-secrets`, `git diff --check`, full deploy to API `6c8a5df3-1956-4a98-ade0-abc6cacf021f`, admin `be596a08-09c6-49db-841f-140772fa34e0`, storefront `8eb6cf3a-d57b-4dd4-bc6d-6486a370f3e5`, live API health/storefront home/dashboard sign-in checks, and live browser `/admin`, `/admin/products`, `/admin/orders`, `/admin/customers`, storefront home smoke with no error-boundary page or captured console errors.
- `CACHE-003` 2026-06-15 follow-up fixed hero slider cache behavior: GET-by-id no longer purges homepage caches, while DELETE now invalidates the homepage group like create/update. Verification: `pnpm --filter @scalius/api exec vitest run src/routes/admin/settings/hero-sliders-cache-invalidation.test.ts src/utils/cache-invalidation.test.ts src/routes/admin/widgets-cache-invalidation.test.ts`, API typecheck/lint, root gates, the same full deploy to API `e1b0a003-9c47-4e7c-9afd-47a209ed3095`, admin `bc36bca6-0628-47dc-8cab-24c6c3bdf9f9`, storefront `d3b9d1e9-795c-44be-99f0-5ab1ae5d7e89`, and live dashboard/storefront smoke.
- `AUTH-011` re-audit on 2026-06-15 originally found trusted-device and preferred-method gaps; the later 2026-06-15 hardening slice verified the current conservative policy by rejecting trusted-device attempts at both boundaries and using pending Better Auth method hints. Re-enabling remembered-device behavior still requires a separate future tracker item.
- `BUILD-003` fixed by adding `scripts/clean-dist-env-files.mjs`, running it after admin/storefront framework builds, excluding local env files from Turbo build outputs, excluding ignored OS/generated files from Turbo inputs, routing package-local deploy scripts through the root safety wrapper, and making `scripts/deploy.mjs` fail before deploy if any app `dist/` contains `.dev.vars`, `.env*`, or `*.vars`. Wrangler dry-runs for admin and storefront did not include the pre-existing `.dev.vars` files in uploaded artifacts, but the build invariant is now explicit and enforced. Verification: `node --check scripts/clean-dist-env-files.mjs`, `node --check scripts/deploy.mjs`, `node scripts/clean-dist-env-files.mjs apps/admin-v2 apps/storefront`, `node scripts/clean-dist-env-files.mjs --check apps/admin-v2 apps/storefront apps/api`, `pnpm exec vitest run scripts/clean-dist-env-files.test.mjs`, `pnpm --filter @scalius/admin-v2 build`, `pnpm --filter @scalius/storefront build`, `pnpm check:dist-secrets`, and `pnpm exec turbo run build --dry=json --filter=@scalius/storefront`.
- `AUTH-001` fixed by mapping Better Auth's `twoFactorVerified` additional session field to the Drizzle schema key instead of the physical SQL column name, allowing `/api/auth/sign-in/email` to create sessions again. The first-admin setup route now releases its KV creation lock in `finally` and can complete a partially-created first user when Better Auth already inserted the account but no admin exists. `/admin` now runs its guard during SSR so unauthenticated requests server-redirect to `/auth/login` instead of hydrating from an admin pending shell into the auth layout. Chart containers now start Recharts with a valid initial size. - `ORDER-009` fixed by making admin full order edits fail closed on reserved/deducted negative-delta failures, moving release/restore deltas before order item replacement, sharing the central shipped/delivered inventory predicate, batching item delete+insert so insert failure cannot erase old item context, and compensating pre-write inventory deltas if later writes fail. Verification: `pnpm exec vitest run tests/unit/core/orders/update-order-atomicity.test.ts`, `pnpm --filter @scalius/core exec vitest run src/modules/orders/orders.fulfillment.test.ts src/modules/delivery/tracking.test.ts src/modules/payments/process-payment.test.ts`, and `pnpm --filter @scalius/core typecheck`.
- `ORDER-010` fixed by removing the shared-batch-failure pattern that released all reservations best-effort and then re-reserved each isolated order. The fallback now re-checks whether the shared D1 batch actually committed an order, reuses the reservation already acquired for isolated replay, releases only that order's original reservation if isolated writes fail, treats `releaseMultiple()` returning `success: false` as rollback failure, and fails checkout closed for manual reconciliation instead of retrying into a second reservation when release is uncertain. Verification: `pnpm --filter @scalius/core test -- src/modules/orders/orders.queue.test.ts`, `pnpm exec vitest run tests/unit/core/orders/order-ingest-queue.test.ts`, `pnpm exec vitest run tests/unit/core/payments/process-payment.test.ts`, `pnpm --filter @scalius/core typecheck`, `pnpm --filter @scalius/core lint`, root `pnpm typecheck`, root `pnpm lint`, root `pnpm test`, `pnpm run deploy:api` to API version `b361f707-6450-42f0-9f88-a80d0b799d14`, live HTTP checks for API/dashboard/storefront, and browser checks for dashboard/storefront with no console errors.
- `ORDER-011` fixed by making `restoreOrder()` apply an explicit trash-restore inventory matrix instead of blindly re-reserving every `inventoryAction = "restored"` order. Incomplete/pending/processing/confirmed orders re-reserve variant inventory and become `reserved`, or become `none` when no variant inventory exists; cancelled/returned/refunded stay `restored`; invalid existing `reserved`/`deducted` pairs reject; shipped/delivered/completed/partially-refunded restored orders reject until deliberate inventory/status reconciliation is performed. Successful re-reservations are released if the final restore CAS fails. `buildInventoryStatements()` now re-reserves only `isStockReservableStatus()` statuses. Verification: `pnpm exec vitest run tests/unit/core/orders/update-order-atomicity.test.ts`, `pnpm exec vitest run tests/unit/core/inventory/reserve-deduct-release.test.ts`, `pnpm --filter @scalius/core typecheck`, `pnpm --filter @scalius/core lint`, root `pnpm typecheck`, root `pnpm lint`, root `pnpm test` at the then-current suite size, `pnpm run deploy:api` to API version `c36bc4ca-bccf-4276-9be9-5c0f86e562ea`, live HTTP checks for API/dashboard/storefront, direct demo email sign-in/session check, and browser checks for dashboard orders/storefront with no console errors.
- `DEL-002` fixed by centralizing shipment-delete safety in `deleteShipmentRecord()`: `creating` and `reconcile_required` rows reject, any active order shipment claim rejects, expired matching claims reject unless the shipment is failed/cancelled, and stale failed/cancelled matching claims are cleared before the shipment row is deleted. Verification: `pnpm --filter @scalius/core exec vitest run src/modules/delivery/delivery.service.test.ts`, `pnpm --filter @scalius/core typecheck`, `pnpm --filter @scalius/core lint`, root `pnpm typecheck`, root `pnpm lint`, root `pnpm test` at the then-current suite size, `pnpm run deploy:api` to API version `5a206ef1-adf4-42f3-bcab-ffe13c7d1e40`, live HTTP checks for API/dashboard/storefront, direct demo email sign-in/session check, and browser checks for dashboard orders/storefront with no console errors.

Cart validation summary copy now keeps the server/cart handoff message for one validation pass only, renders bulk repair buttons for removable, reducible, and price-refreshable issue groups, and clears repaired line warnings immediately while final validation stays authoritative. API order policy now treats a forwarded customer session as authoritative even when guests are allowed, rejects signed-in phone mismatches, and returns `CUSTOMER_SESSION_STALE` for expired sessions so the storefront proxy clears `cs_tok`/`cs_auth` before retry. Storefront order building no longer resolves customers from submitted phone; authenticated payloads carry the session `customerId`, true guest payloads keep `existingCustomer: null`, and commit fresh-reads the active customer before setting `orders.customerId`.
